## Scope and limits

- GitHub: authenticated `SouthpawIN` repository list plus public/default README snapshots.
- Hugging Face: public model, dataset, and space metadata/cards for `sovthpaw` and `Senter-Alignment`.
- Discord: the locally available `projects/nous-discord-archive`; live Discord channel history was not queried by this run.
- Local: authored text-like files under `projects/`, `docs/`, `research/`, `.hermes/wiki/`, `lyrics/`, and `notes/`, excluding dependencies, generated/model artifacts, secrets, and binaries.
- Sessions: user/assistant prose from local session JSON, with credential-like strings redacted.
- Redundancy: exact normalized duplicates are collapsed; semantic near-duplicates still require review.
